# RAS flavor
# by Silverswee(e)per

namespace = soh

# IDs 12301-12800 reserved



##### Flavour events

### Hospitals and hospital buildings in own counties

# Construct Hospital?
character_event = {
	id = soh.12301
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12301
	picture = GFX_evt_castle_construction
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	has_dlc = "Reapers"
	has_dlc = "Mystics"
	only_playable = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	min_age = 16
	
	trigger = {
		society_member_of = the_alchemists
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		any_demesne_province = {
			has_hospital = no
		}
		society_currency >= 100
		wealth >= 50
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_demesne_province = {
			limit = {
				has_hospital = no
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = hospital_province
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12301 # Sure, let's build it!
		
		wealth = -50
		change_society_currency = -100
		
		event_target:hospital_province = {
			build_holding = {
				type = hospital
				holder = ROOT
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = greedy
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = cruel
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				capital_scope = {
					NOT = {
						province = event_target:hospital_province
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				wealth < 100
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				society_currency < 200
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12301 # I don't particularly want it...
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = kind
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = charitable
			}
		}
	}
}

# Construct Sick House?
character_event = {
	id = soh.12302
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12302
	picture = GFX_evt_castle_construction
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	has_dlc = "Reapers"
	has_dlc = "Mystics"
	only_playable = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	min_age = 16
	
	trigger = {
		society_member_of = the_alchemists
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		any_demesne_province = {
			has_hospital = yes
			NOT = {
				hospital_has_building = hospital_building_1
			}
			TECH_CONSTRUCTION >= 2
		}
		society_currency >= 250
		wealth >= 125
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_demesne_province = {
			limit = {
				has_hospital = yes
				NOT = {
					hospital_has_building = hospital_building_1
				}
				TECH_CONSTRUCTION >= 2
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = hospital_province
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12302 # Sure, let's build it!
		
		wealth = -125
		change_society_currency = -250
		
		event_target:hospital_province = {
			add_to_extra_holding  = {
				type = hospital
				building = hospital_building_1
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = greedy
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = cruel
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				capital_scope = {
					NOT = {
						province = event_target:hospital_province
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				wealth < 250
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				society_currency < 500
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12302 # I don't particularly want it...
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = kind
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = charitable
			}
		}
	}
}

				hospital_has_building = hospital_building_2

				type = hospital

				factor = 0.1
				trait = greedy
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = cruel
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				capital_scope = {
					NOT = {
						province = event_target:hospital_province
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				wealth < 500
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				society_currency < 1000
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12303 # I don't particularly want it...
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100

				factor = 0.1
				trait = kind

# Construct Leper Colony?
character_event = {
	id = soh.12307
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12307
	picture = GFX_evt_castle_construction
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	has_dlc = "Reapers"
	has_dlc = "Mystics"
	only_playable = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	min_age = 16
	
	trigger = {
		society_member_of = the_alchemists
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		any_demesne_province = {
			has_hospital = yes
			hospital_has_building = hospital_building_1
			NOT = {
				hospital_has_building = leper_colony_1
			}
			TECH_CONSTRUCTION >= 2
		}
		society_currency >= 300
		wealth >= 150
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_demesne_province = {
			limit = {
				has_hospital = yes
				hospital_has_building = hospital_building_1
				NOT = {
					hospital_has_building = leper_colony_1

				factor = 0.5

			}

# Construct Pilgrim's Inn?
character_event = {
	id = soh.12311
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12311
	picture = GFX_evt_castle_construction
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	has_dlc = "Reapers"
	has_dlc = "Mystics"
	only_playable = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	min_age = 16
	
	trigger = {
		society_member_of = the_alchemists
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		any_demesne_province = {
			has_hospital = yes
			hospital_has_building = hospital_building_1
			NOT = {
				hospital_has_building = pilgrims_inn_1
			}
			TECH_CONSTRUCTION >= 3
		}
		society_currency >= 400
		wealth >= 200
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_demesne_province = {
			limit = {
				has_hospital = yes
				hospital_has_building = hospital_building_1
				NOT = {
					hospital_has_building = pilgrims_inn_1
				}
				TECH_CONSTRUCTION >= 3
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = hospital_province
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12311 # Sure, let's build it!
		
		wealth = -200
		change_society_currency = -400

			add_to_extra_holding  = {
				type = hospital
				building = pilgrims_inn_1
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {

			}

		society_member_of = the_alchemists
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
	}

		random_list = {
			100 = {
				trigger = {
					is_playable = no
				}

					factor = 0.1

character_event = {
	id = soh.12317
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12317
	picture = GFX_evt_market
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_economy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12317 # I'll take some of that!
		
		wealth = -10
		add_artifact = ingredient_herb1
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			stewardship >= 10
		}
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12317 # How about a bulk discount?
		
		wealth = -25
		add_artifact = ingredient_herb1
		add_artifact = ingredient_herb1
		add_artifact = ingredient_herb1
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTC_SOH_12317 # Not interested!
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOT = {
					has_character_flag = immortality_potion_needs_ingredient_herb1
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				NOT = {
					any_artifact = {
						has_artifact_flag = ingredient
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

		add_society_currency_tiny_effect = yes
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOT = {
					has_character_flag = immortality_potion_needs_ingredient_mineral_jade_2
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12415 # I need all I have...
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = charitable
			}
		}
	}
}

character_event = {
	id = soh.12420
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	has_dlc = "Mystics"
	is_in_society = yes
	prisoner = no
	min_age = 16
	
	trigger = {
		society_member_of = the_alchemists
		is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
		OR = {
			learning >= 15
			AND = {
				trait = erudite
				learning >= 10
			}
			AND = {
				trait = scholar
				learning >= 10
			}
			any_artifact = {
				has_artifact_flag = book
				has_artifact_flag = alchemy
				has_artifact_flag = taoist
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {

			100 = {

character_event = {
	id = soh.12421
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12421
	picture = GFX_evt_scientist
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12421 # Knowledge is power!
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			change_variable = {
				which = ingredient_herb1_knowledge
				value = 1
			}
			
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					change_variable = {
						which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
						value = 2
					}
				}
				
				50 = {
					change_variable = {
						which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
						value = 1
					}
					change_variable = {
						which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
						value = 1
					}
				}
				
				25 = {
					change_variable = {
						which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
						value = 2
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12425
	picture = GFX_evt_scientist
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12425 # Knowledge is power!
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			change_variable = {
				which = ingredient_herb5_knowledge
				value = 1
			}
			
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					change_variable = {
						which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
						value = 2
					}
				}
				
				50 = {
					change_variable = {
						which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
						value = 1
					}
					change_variable = {
						which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
						value = 1
					}
				}
				
				25 = {
					change_variable = {
						which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
						value = 2
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

		}
	}
}

### Humiliating potion

# Brew humiliating potion?
character_event = {
	id = soh.12472
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12472
	picture = GFX_evt_rival_rulers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	has_dlc = "Mystics"
	is_in_society = yes
	min_age = 16
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		society_member_of = the_alchemists
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		can_brew_potion_trigger = yes
		
		top_liege = {
			any_realm_character = {
				is_adult = yes
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				OR = {
					is_rival = ROOT
					reverse_opinion = {
						who = ROOT
						value < 0
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		any_artifact = {
			count >= 2
			has_artifact_flag = ingredient
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		top_liege = {
			random_realm_character = {
				limit = {
					is_adult = yes
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					OR = {
						is_rival = ROOT
						reverse_opinion = {
							who = ROOT
							value < 0
						}
					}
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					employer = {
						OR = {
							any_courtier = {
								character = ROOT
							}
							character = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
				
				save_event_target_as = bad_potion_target
			}
		}
		
		set_character_flag = considering_potion
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12472 # I'll go for it!
		
		set_character_flag = brewing_potion
		
		clear_flags_with_prefix = created_humiliating_potion_is_
		clear_flags_with_prefix = humiliating_potion_is_
		
		set_character_flag = humiliating_potion_is_helpful
		
		set_character_flag = getting_potion
		
		character_event = { id = soh.12740 days = 30 } # Cleanup
		
		character_event = { id = soh.12473 days = 1 } # Decide on ingredients
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					trait = kind
					trait = honest
					trait = just
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = kind
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = honest
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = just
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				opinion = {
					who = event_target:bad_potion_target
					value < -25
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 5
				opinion = {
					who = event_target:bad_potion_target
					value < -50
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12472 # I don't feel like it!
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					trait = cruel
					trait = arbitrary
					trait = deceitful
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = cruel
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = arbitrary
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = deceitful
			}
		}
	}
	
	after = {
		clr_character_flag = considering_potion
	}
}

# Which ingredients to use?
character_event = {
	id = soh.12473
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12473
	picture = GFX_evt_experiment
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		remove_selection_of_ingredients_effect = yes
		select_random_hermetic_ingredient_effect = yes
		select_random_hermetic_ingredient_effect = yes
	}

	option = { # I will use ingredient 1
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12473
		
		calculate_health_level_of_ingredient_1_effect = yes
		save_ingredient_1_flag_humiliating_potion_effect = yes
		destroy_selected_ingredient_1_effect = yes
	}

	option = { # I will use ingredient 2
		trigger = {
			has_selected_ingredient_2_trigger = yes
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12473
		
		calculate_health_level_of_ingredient_2_effect = yes
		save_ingredient_2_flag_humiliating_potion_effect = yes
		destroy_selected_ingredient_2_effect = yes
	}

	option = { # I will use both!
		trigger = {
			has_selected_ingredient_2_trigger = yes
			selected_ingredient_1_and_2_are_same = no
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTC_SOH_12473
		
		calculate_health_level_of_ingredient_1_and_2_effect = yes
		save_ingredient_1_and_2_flag_humiliating_potion_effect = yes
		destroy_selected_ingredient_1_and_2_effect = yes
	}
	
	after = {
		hidden_tooltip = {
			set_variable = {
				which = alchemist_humiliating_potion_quality
				which = local_current_potion_quality
			}
			
			# Alchemist's knowledge matters a bit
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
						value >= 100
					}
				}
				
				change_variable = {
					which = alchemist_humiliating_potion_quality
					value = -4
				}
			}
			
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
						value >= 75
					}
				}
				
				change_variable = {
					which = alchemist_humiliating_potion_quality
					value = -3
				}
			}
			
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
						value >= 50
					}
				}
				
				change_variable = {
					which = alchemist_humiliating_potion_quality
					value = -2
				}
			}
			
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
						value >= 25
					}
				}
				
				change_variable = {
					which = alchemist_humiliating_potion_quality
					value = -1
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = soh.12474 days = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# Potion is ready
character_event = {
	id = soh.12474
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12474
	picture = GFX_evt_experiment
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		clr_character_flag = brewing_potion
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12474 # Now to deliver it to the target...
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			set_variable = {
				which = humiliating_potion_to_deliver
				value = 0
			}
			
			while = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						is_variable_equal = {
							which = alchemist_humiliating_potion_quality
							value = 0
						}
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							which = alchemist_humiliating_potion_quality
							value > 0
						}
					}
					
					change_variable = {
						which = alchemist_humiliating_potion_quality
						value = -0.05
					}
					
					change_variable = {
						which = humiliating_potion_to_deliver
						value = 0.05
					}
				}
				
				else = {
					change_variable = {
						which = alchemist_humiliating_potion_quality
						value = 0.05
					}
					
					change_variable = {
						which = humiliating_potion_to_deliver
						value = -0.05
					}
				}
			}
			
			copy_humiliating_potion_flags_effect = yes
		}
		
		character_event = { id = soh.11700 days = 5 random = 10 } # Determine if the delivery is successful
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12474 # Actually, let's not...
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0
		}
	}
	
	after = {
		clr_character_flag = getting_potion
		
		clear_delayed_event = { id = soh.12740 } # Do not fire the cleanup event
	}
}

	id = soh.12475
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12475

			who = FROMFROM
			name = opinion_attempted_potion_plot
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					is_rival = FROMFROM
					is_friend = FROM
					is_lover = FROM
					is_close_relative = FROM
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = just
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			FROMFROM = {
				is_landed = no
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12475 # Guards! Seize them!
		
		opinion = {
			who = FROMFROM
			name = opinion_attempted_potion_plot
		}
		
		FROMFROM = {
			imprison = ROOT
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					is_friend = FROMFROM
					is_lover = FROMFROM
					AND = {
						is_close_relative = FROMFROM
						NOT = {
							is_rival = FROMFROM
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = arbitrary
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				is_rival = FROM
			}
		}
	}
}

						value < 0
					}

		}

								character = ROOT
							}
							character = ROOT
						}
					}

				value >= 50
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12476 # I'll go for it in a more sneaky fashion!
		
		set_character_flag = brewing_potion
		
		clear_flags_with_prefix = created_poison_is_
		
		set_character_flag = created_poison_is_sneaky_harmful
		
		set_character_flag = getting_potion

# Which ingredients to use? - normal poison
character_event = {
	id = soh.12477
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12477
	picture = GFX_evt_experiment
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		remove_selection_of_ingredients_effect = yes
		select_random_hermetic_ingredient_prefer_harmful_effect = yes
		select_random_hermetic_ingredient_prefer_harmful_effect = yes
	}

	option = { # I will use ingredient 1
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12477
		
		calculate_health_level_of_ingredient_1_effect = yes
		save_ingredient_1_flag_poison_effect = yes
		destroy_selected_ingredient_1_effect = yes
	}

	option = { # I will use ingredient 2
		trigger = {
			has_selected_ingredient_2_trigger = yes
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12477
		
		calculate_health_level_of_ingredient_2_effect = yes
		save_ingredient_2_flag_poison_effect = yes
		destroy_selected_ingredient_2_effect = yes
	}

	option = { # I will use both!
		trigger = {
			has_selected_ingredient_2_trigger = yes
			selected_ingredient_1_and_2_are_same = no
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTC_SOH_12477
		
		calculate_health_level_of_ingredient_1_and_2_effect = yes
		save_ingredient_1_and_2_flag_poison_effect = yes
		destroy_selected_ingredient_1_and_2_effect = yes
	}
	
	after = {
		hidden_tooltip = {
			set_variable = {
				which = alchemist_poison_quality
				which = local_current_potion_quality
			}
			
			# Alchemist's knowledge matters a bit
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
						value >= 100
					}
				}
				
				change_variable = {
					which = alchemist_poison_quality
					value = -4
				}
			}
			
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
						value >= 75
					}
				}
				
				change_variable = {
					which = alchemist_poison_quality
					value = -3
				}
			}
			
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
						value >= 50
					}
				}
				
				change_variable = {
					which = alchemist_poison_quality
					value = -2
				}
			}
			
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
						value >= 25
					}
				}
				
				change_variable = {
					which = alchemist_poison_quality
					value = -1
				}
			}
			
			random = {
				chance = 1
				character_event = { id = soh.12733 days = 1 } # Dumb assistant asks about poison
			}
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						religion_openly_aztec_or_reformed_trigger = yes
						has_religion_feature = religion_ritual_sacrifice
					}
				}
				
				random = {
					chance = 1
					character_event = { id = soh.12734 days = 1 } # Poison might need more... body
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = soh.12479 days = 2 } # Poison is ready
		}
	}
}

character_event = {
	id = soh.12479
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12479
	picture = GFX_evt_experiment
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		clear_flags_with_prefix = poison_is_
		trigger_switch = {
			on_trigger = has_character_flag
			
			created_poison_is_harmful = { ROOT = { set_character_flag = poison_is_harmful } }
			created_poison_is_sneaky_harmful = { ROOT = { set_character_flag = poison_is_sneaky_harmful } }
		}
		
		clr_character_flag = brewing_potion
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12479 # Now to deliver it to the target...
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			set_variable = {
				which = poison_to_deliver
				value = 0
			}
			
			while = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						is_variable_equal = {
							which = alchemist_poison_quality
							value = 0
						}
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							which = alchemist_poison_quality
							value > 0
						}
					}
					
					change_variable = {
						which = alchemist_poison_quality
						value = -0.05
					}
					
					change_variable = {
						which = poison_to_deliver
						value = 0.05
					}
				}
				
				else = {
					change_variable = {
						which = alchemist_poison_quality
						value = 0.05
					}
					
					change_variable = {
						which = poison_to_deliver
						value = -0.05
					}
				}
			}
			
			copy_poison_flags_effect = yes
		}
		
		character_event = { id = soh.11761 days = 5 random = 10 } # Determine if the delivery is successful
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12479 # Actually, let's not...
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0
		}
	}
	
	after = {
		clr_character_flag = getting_potion
		
		clear_delayed_event = { id = soh.12740 } # Do not fire the cleanup event
	}
}

# Employer of target informed of failed attempt
character_event = {
	id = soh.12480
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12480
	picture = GFX_evt_feast
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12480 # Perhaps this warrants imprisonment...
		
		opinion = {
			who = FROMFROM
			name = opinion_attempted_potion_plot
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					is_rival = FROMFROM
					is_friend = FROM
					is_lover = FROM
					is_close_relative = FROM
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = just
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			FROMFROM = {
				is_landed = no
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12480 # Guards! Seize them!
		
		opinion = {
			who = FROMFROM
			name = opinion_attempted_potion_plot
		}
		
		FROMFROM = {
			imprison = ROOT
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					is_friend = FROMFROM
					is_lover = FROMFROM
					AND = {
						is_close_relative = FROMFROM
						NOT = {
							is_rival = FROMFROM
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = arbitrary
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				is_rival = FROM
			}
		}
	}
}

character_event = {

	hide_window = yes

			100 = {

### Learning discussion

# Learning discussion - participate?
character_event = {
	id = soh.12497
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12497
	picture = GFX_evt_gathering_of_minds
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	only_playable = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	min_age = 16
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		society_member_of = the_alchemists
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12497 # I'll participate!
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					character_event = { id = soh.12498 days = 5 random = 5 } # Gain prestige
				}
				
				25 = {
					character_event = { id = soh.12499 days = 5 random = 5 } # Gain society currency 
				}
				
				10 = {
					trigger = {
						OR = {
							trait = scholarly_theologian
							trait = dutiful_cleric
							trait = detached_priest
						}
					}
					
					character_event = { id = soh.12500 days = 5 random = 5 } # Improve education trait
				}
				
				50 = {
					trigger = {
						NOT = {
							trait = mastermind_theologian
						}
					}
					
					character_event = { id = soh.12501 days = 5 random = 5 } # Discussion goes okay
				}
				
				25 = {
					trigger = {
						NOT = {
							trait = mastermind_theologian
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.1
						trait = scholarly_theologian
					}
					
					character_event = { id = soh.12502 days = 5 random = 5 } # Lose prestige
				}
				
				25 = {
					trigger = {
						NOT = {
							trait = mastermind_theologian
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.1
						trait = scholarly_theologian
					}
					
					character_event = { id = soh.12503 days = 5 random = 5 } # Lose society currency
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12497 # I think not!
		
		detract_society_currency_tiny_effect = yes
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0
		}
	}
}

### Medicine discussion

# Medicine discussion - participate?
character_event = {
	id = soh.12504
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12504
	picture = GFX_evt_gathering_of_minds
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	is_in_society = yes
	min_age = 16
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		society_member_of = the_alchemists
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12504 # I'll participate!
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						check_variable = {
							which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
							value >= 25
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						check_variable = {
							which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
							value >= 50
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						check_variable = {
							which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
							value >= 75
						}
					}
					
					character_event = { id = soh.12505 days = 5 random = 5 } # Gain prestige
				}
				
				25 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						check_variable = {
							which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
							value >= 25
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						check_variable = {
							which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
							value >= 50
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						check_variable = {
							which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
							value >= 75
						}
					}
					
					character_event = { id = soh.12506 days = 5 random = 5 } # Gain society currency 
				}
				
				25 = {
					trigger = {
						check_variable = {
							which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
							value < 100
						}
					}
					
					character_event = { id = soh.12507 days = 5 random = 5 } # Gain healing insights
				}
				
				50 = {
					trigger = {
						check_variable = {
							which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
							value < 100
						}
					}
					
					character_event = { id = soh.12508 days = 5 random = 5 } # Discussion goes okay
				}
				
				25 = {
					trigger = {
						check_variable = {
							which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
							value < 50
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						check_variable = {
							which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
							value < 25
						}
					}
					
					character_event = { id = soh.12509 days = 5 random = 5 } # Lose prestige
				}
				
				25 = {
					trigger = {
						check_variable = {
							which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
							value < 50
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						check_variable = {
							which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
							value < 25
						}
					}
					
					character_event = { id = soh.12510 days = 5 random = 5 } # Lose society currency
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12504 # I think not!
		
		detract_society_currency_tiny_effect = yes
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0
		}
	}
}

			has_character_modifier = cheap_laboratory

		}

### Mad scientist abducting new test subject - based on MNM.20070 and follow-ups

# Lunatic with no prisoner to experiment on and access to prisoner experiments might attempt an abduction
character_event = {
	id = soh.12527
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12527
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	has_dlc = "Mystics"
	only_rulers = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	min_age = 16
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		society_member_of = the_alchemists
		
		NOT = {
			any_courtier = {
				prisoner = yes
			}
		}
		
		OR = {
			trait = lunatic
			trait = possessed
		}
		
		employer = {
			top_liege = {
				any_realm_character = {
					ai = yes # TIANXIATODO: Review down the line
					is_adult = yes
					is_patrician = no
					is_within_diplo_range = FROM

					trigger_if = {
						limit = { is_landed = yes }
						lower_real_tier_than = DUKE
					}

					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							father = { always = yes }
						}

						father_even_if_dead = {
							lower_real_tier_than = DUKE
							is_patrician = no
							ai = yes # TIANXIATODO: Review down the line
						}
					}

					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							mother = { always = yes }
						}

						mother_even_if_dead = {
							lower_real_tier_than = DUKE
							is_patrician = no
							ai = yes # TIANXIATODO: Review down the line
						}
					}
					
					trigger_if = {
						limit = { is_married = yes }

						NOT = {
							any_spouse = {
								OR = {
									higher_real_tier_than = count
									lower_real_tier_than = DUKE
									is_patrician = no
									ai = yes # TIANXIATODO: Review down the line
								}
							}
						}
					}
					
					NOT = { war_with = ROOT }
					NOT = { same_society_as = ROOT }
					prisoner = no
					
					NOR = {
						is_friend = ROOT
						is_lover = ROOT
						AND = {
							is_close_relative = ROOT
							NOT = {
								is_rival = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		NOT = {
			has_any_opinion_modifier = opinion_attempted_abduction
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		employer = {
			top_liege = {
				random_realm_character = {
					limit = {
						ai = yes # TIANXIATODO: Review down the line
						is_patrician = no
						is_within_diplo_range = FROM

						trigger_if = {
							limit = { is_landed = yes }
							lower_real_tier_than = DUKE
						}

						trigger_if = {
							limit = {
								father = { always = yes }
							}

							father_even_if_dead = {
								lower_real_tier_than = DUKE
								is_patrician = no
								ai = yes # TIANXIATODO: Review down the line
							}
						}

						trigger_if = {
							limit = {
								mother = { always = yes }
							}

							mother_even_if_dead = {
								lower_real_tier_than = DUKE
								is_patrician = no
								ai = yes # TIANXIATODO: Review down the line
							}
						}
						
						trigger_if = {
							limit = { is_married = yes }

							NOT = {
								any_spouse = {
									OR = {
										higher_real_tier_than = count
										lower_real_tier_than = DUKE
										is_patrician = no
										ai = yes # TIANXIATODO: Review down the line
									}
								}
							}
						}
						
						NOT = { war_with = ROOT }
						NOT = { same_society_as = ROOT }
						prisoner = no
						
						NOR = {
							is_friend = ROOT
							is_lover = ROOT
							AND = {
								is_close_relative = ROOT
								NOT = {
									is_rival = ROOT
								}
							}
						}
					}
					
					preferred_limit = {
						is_rival = ROOT
					}
					
					preferred_limit = {
						reverse_opinion = {
							who = ROOT
							value < -75
						}
					}
					
					preferred_limit = {
						reverse_opinion = {
							who = ROOT
							value < -50
						}
					}
					
					preferred_limit = {
						reverse_opinion = {
							who = ROOT
							value < -25
						}
					}
					
					preferred_limit = {
						reverse_opinion = {
							who = ROOT
							value < 0
						}
					}
					
					save_event_target_as = abduction_target
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12527 # I do need a test subject!

		character_event = {
			id = soh.12528
			days = 20
			random = 20
			tooltip = EVTTOOLTIP_SOH_12527
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = kind
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = honest
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = just
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = content
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12527 # I think I'll find another test subject elsewhere...
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOT = {
					is_rival = event_target:abduction_target
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = cruel
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = deceitful
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = arbitrary
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = ambitious
			}
		}
	}
}


# Hidden 'outcome tombola' event for the alchemist abduction
character_event = {
	id = soh.12528

	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	#only_capable = yes
	prisoner = no

	trigger = {
		society_member_of = the_alchemists
		OR = {
			trait = lunatic
			trait = possessed
		}

		has_opinion_modifier = {
			who = event_target:abduction_target
			name = opinion_abducting
		}
		is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = {
		character_event = { id = soh.12533 } # Clear out any flags, etc
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:abduction_target = {
					is_alive = no
				}
			}

			character_event = { id = soh.12534 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:abduction_target = {
					prisoner = yes
				}
			}

			character_event = { id = soh.12535 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:abduction_target = {
					same_society_as = ROOT
				}
			}

			character_event = { id = soh.12537 }
		}
		else = {
			repeat_event = { # Fire again with no scope changes
				id = soh.12528
				days = 30
				random = 30
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					event_target:abduction_target = {
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						#is_pilgrim = no # Unnecessary, and would protect the Tenno...
					}
				}

				random_list = {
					10000 = { # No luck yet
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 0.75
							has_character_modifier = wol_schemer_1
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 0.5
							has_character_modifier = wol_schemer_2
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 0.2
							trait = master_schemer
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 0.5
							event_target:abduction_target = { trait = trusting }
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 1.5
							event_target:abduction_target = { has_character_modifier = wol_schemer_1 }
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 3.0
							event_target:abduction_target = { has_character_modifier = wol_schemer_2 }
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 5.0
							event_target:abduction_target = { trait = master_schemer }
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 3.0
							event_target:abduction_target = { trait = paranoid }
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 1.5
							event_target:abduction_target = { real_tier = BARON }
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2.0
							event_target:abduction_target = { real_tier = COUNT }
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 3 # Makes sure it's very hard to abduct the same person twice
							OR = {
								reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
									name = opinion_attempted_abduction
									who = event_target:abduction_target
								}
								reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
									name = opinion_abducted
									who = event_target:abduction_target
								}
							}
						}
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 0.25
							event_target:abduction_target = {
								is_ruler = no
								vassal_of = ROOT
							}
						}

						random = {
							chance = 25
							character_event = { id = soh.12536 } # Time to give up?
						}
					}

					10000 = { # Success!
						character_event = { id = soh.12529 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Chance to abduct the target!

	picture = GFX_evt_bandits
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

		event_target:abduction_target = {
			show_scope_change = no

			character_event = {
				id = soh.12530
				days = 3
				tooltip = EVTTOOLTIP_SOH_12529
			}
		}

		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12529 # Not yet...
	}
}

# Target gets abducted... or not.
character_event = {
	id = soh.12530
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12530
	picture = GFX_evt_bandits
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random = {
			chance = 75

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1 # Makes sure it's very hard to abduct the same person twice
				OR = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = FROM
						name = opinion_attempted_abduction
					}

					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = FROM
						name = opinion_abducted
					}
				}
			}
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				age >= 14

				OR = {
					has_character_modifier = seduced_loyal_bodyguard
					has_character_modifier = seduced_loyal_spy_maid
				}
			}
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2.0
				trait = trusting
			}

			set_character_flag = abduction_success
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPT_WOL_2121_CAUGHT # CleanSlate: target_snooper doesn't exist, but also players can't be the target of the abduct decision, so all the localisation in this event is probably superfluous anyway.

		trigger = { has_character_flag = abduction_success }

		imprison = FROM

		opinion = {
			name = opinion_abducted
			who = FROM
			years = 50
		}

		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = soh.12531 }
		}

		clr_character_flag = abduction_success
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPT_WOL_2121

		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = abduction_success } }

		if = {
			limit = {
				is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
				age >= 4

				OR = {
					trait = paranoid
					random < 50
				}
			}

			custom_tooltip = { text = EVTTOOLTIP_SOH_12530_PERP }

			opinion = {
				name = opinion_attempted_abduction
				who = FROM
				years = 10
			}
		}

		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = soh.12532 }
		}
	}
}

# Target gets abducted!

# Abduction fails
character_event = {
	id = soh.12532
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12532
	picture = GFX_evt_bandits
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12532 # Idiots!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc = "Jade Dragon"
				e_china = {
					holder_scope = {
						is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
						OR = {
							character = event_target:abduction_target
							AND = {
								is_close_relative = event_target:abduction_target
								dynasty = event_target:abduction_target
							}
						}
						NOT = {
							character = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					event_target:abduction_target = {
						is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
					}
				}
				detract_grace_major_effect = yes
			}
			else = {
				detract_grace_medium_effect = yes
			}
		}

		tooltip = {
			reverse_opinion = {
				name = opinion_attempted_abduction
				who = event_target:abduction_target
				years = 10
			}
		}
	}
}

### Attempt to one-up rival alchemist

# Opportunity to one-up rival alchemist
character_event = {
	id = soh.12538
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12538
	picture = GFX_evt_rival_rulers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	has_dlc = "Mystics"
	only_rulers = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	min_age = 16
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		society_member_of = the_alchemists
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		any_rival = {
			society_member_of = the_alchemists
			is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
			NOT = {
				has_character_flag = alchemist_one_up_attempt_ongoing
			}
		}
		
		OR = {
			check_variable = {
				which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
				value >= 10
			}
			check_variable = {
				which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
				value >= 10
			}
		}
		
		any_artifact = {
			count >= 2
			has_artifact_flag = ingredient
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_rival = {
			limit = {
				society_member_of = the_alchemists
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				NOT = {
					has_character_flag = alchemist_one_up_attempt_ongoing
				}
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
					who = ROOT
					modifier = opinion_one_upped_me
				}
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				has_opinion_modifier = {
					who = ROOT
					modifier = opinion_did_not_one_up_me
				}
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = alchemist_to_one_up
		}
		
		random_artifact = {
			limit = {
				has_artifact_flag = ingredient
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = one_up_artifact
		}
		
		clear_flags_with_prefix = alchemist_one_up_attempt_
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			check_variable = {
				which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
				value >= 10
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12538 # I'll brew the best potion ever!
		
		set_character_flag = do_not_disturb
		
		set_character_flag = alchemist_one_up_attempt_ongoing
		
		set_character_flag = alchemist_one_up_attempt_healing
		
		character_event = { id = soh.12539 days = 5 random = 5 } # Determine outcome
		
		character_event = { id = soh.12560 days = 15 } # Flag cleanup
		
		event_target:one_up_artifact = {
			destroy_artifact = yes
		}
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_character_flag = alchemist_one_up_started
					}
				}
				
				set_character_flag = alchemist_one_up_started
				
				set_variable = {
					which = alchemist_one_up
					value = 1
				}
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOT = {
					trait = master_schemer
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = physician
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = stubborn
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = proud
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = diligent
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = kind
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = ambitious
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			check_variable = {
				which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
				value >= 10
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12538 # I'll brew the best poison ever!
		
		set_character_flag = do_not_disturb
		
		set_character_flag = alchemist_one_up_attempt_ongoing
		
		set_character_flag = alchemist_one_up_attempt_poison
		
		character_event = { id = soh.12539 days = 5 random = 5 } # Determine outcome
		
		character_event = { id = soh.12560 days = 15 } # Flag cleanup
		
		event_target:one_up_artifact = {
			destroy_artifact = yes
		}
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_character_flag = alchemist_one_up_started
					}
				}
				
				set_character_flag = alchemist_one_up_started
				
				set_variable = {
					which = alchemist_one_up
					value = 1
				}
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOT = {
					trait = physician
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = master_schemer
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = stubborn
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = proud
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = diligent
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = deceitful
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = ambitious
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTC_SOH_12538 # It'd be a waste of time...
		
		prestige = -50
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = humble
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = content
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = slothful
			}
		}
	}
}

# Determine outcome
character_event = {
	id = soh.12539
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		society_member_of = the_alchemists
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
	}
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:alchemist_to_one_up = {
					has_character_flag = alchemist_one_up_started
				}
			}
			
			event_target:alchemist_to_one_up = {
				set_variable = {
					which = alchemist_one_up_target
					which = alchemist_one_up
				}
			}
			
			set_variable = {
				which = alchemist_one_up_target
				which = event_target:alchemist_to_one_up
			}
		}
		
		else = {
			set_variable = {
				which = alchemist_one_up_target
				value = 1
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = alchemist_one_up_attempt_healing
			}
			
			set_variable = {
				which = alchemist_one_up_attempt
				which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
			}
		}
		
		else = {
			set_variable = {
				which = alchemist_one_up_attempt
				which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_modifier = cheap_laboratory # Better than no laboratory...
			}
			
			multiply_variable = {
				which = alchemist_one_up_attempt
				value = 1.1
			}
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_modifier = normal_laboratory
			}
			
			multiply_variable = {
				which = alchemist_one_up_attempt
				value = 1.2
			}
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_modifier = good_laboratory
			}
			
			multiply_variable = {
				which = alchemist_one_up_attempt
				value = 1.5
			}
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_modifier = great_laboratory
			}
			
			multiply_variable = {
				which = alchemist_one_up_attempt
				value = 1.8
			}
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_modifier = best_laboratory
			}
			
			multiply_variable = {
				which = alchemist_one_up_attempt
				value = 2
			}
		}
		
		divide_variable = {
			which = alchemist_one_up_attempt
			value = 10
		}
		
		# 1d20
		random_list = {
			1 = {
				set_variable = {
					which = alchemist_d20
					value = 100 # Critical success, therefore very likely (but not guaranteed) to succeed
				}
			}
			1 = {
				set_variable = {
					which = alchemist_d20
					value = 19
				}
			}
			1 = {
				set_variable = {
					which = alchemist_d20
					value = 18
				}
			}
			1 = {
				set_variable = {
					which = alchemist_d20
					value = 17
				}
			}
			1 = {
				set_variable = {
					which = alchemist_d20
					value = 16
				}
			}
			1 = {
				set_variable = {
					which = alchemist_d20
					value = 15
				}
			}
			1 = {
				set_variable = {
					which = alchemist_d20
					value = 14
				}
			}
			1 = {
				set_variable = {
					which = alchemist_d20
					value = 13
				}
			}
			1 = {
				set_variable = {
					which = alchemist_d20
					value = 12
				}
			}
			1 = {
				set_variable = {
					which = alchemist_d20
					value = 11
				}
			}
			1 = {
				set_variable = {
					which = alchemist_d20
					value = 10
				}
			}
			1 = {
				set_variable = {
					which = alchemist_d20
					value = 9
				}
			}
			1 = {
				set_variable = {
					which = alchemist_d20
					value = 8
				}
			}
			1 = {
				set_variable = {
					which = alchemist_d20
					value = 7
				}
			}
			1 = {
				set_variable = {
					which = alchemist_d20
					value = 6
				}
			}
			1 = {
				set_variable = {
					which = alchemist_d20
					value = 5
				}
			}
			1 = {
				set_variable = {
					which = alchemist_d20
					value = 4
				}
			}
			1 = {
				set_variable = {
					which = alchemist_d20
					value = 3
				}
			}
			1 = {
				set_variable = {
					which = alchemist_d20
					value = 2
				}
			}
			1 = {
				set_variable = {
					which = alchemist_d20
					value = -1000 # Critical failure
				}
			}
		}
		
		divide_variable = {
			which = alchemist_d20
			value = 20
		}
		
		change_variable = {
			which = alchemist_one_up_attempt
			which = alchemist_d20
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = alchemist_one_up_attempt_healing
			}
			
			export_to_variable = {
				which = alchemist_one_up_stat
				value = learning
			}
		}
		
		else = {
			export_to_variable = {
				which = alchemist_one_up_stat
				value = intrigue
			}
		}
		
		divide_variable = {
			which = alchemist_one_up_stat
			value = 100
		}
		
		change_variable = {
			which = alchemist_one_up_attempt
			which = alchemist_one_up_stat
		}
		
		set_variable = {
			which = alchemist_one_up_result
			which = alchemist_one_up_attempt
		}
		
		subtract_variable = {
			which = alchemist_one_up_result
			which = alchemist_one_up_target
		}
		
		# Determine the outcome
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					which = alchemist_one_up_result
					value < 0
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = soh.12540 } # Killed in lab accident
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					which = alchemist_one_up_result
					value < 0.1
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = soh.12542 } # Maimed in lab accident
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					which = alchemist_one_up_result
					value < 0.2
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = soh.12544 } # Wounded in lab accident
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					which = alchemist_one_up_result
					value < 0.4
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = soh.12546 } # Damaged lab equipment
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					which = alchemist_one_up_result
					value < 0.6
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = soh.12548 } # High on fumes
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					which = alchemist_one_up_result
					value < 0.8
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = soh.12550 } # Unremarkable failure
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					which = alchemist_one_up_result
					value < 1.2
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = soh.12552 } # Average outcome
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					which = alchemist_one_up_result
					value < 1.6
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = soh.12554 } # Unremarkable victory
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					which = alchemist_one_up_result
					value < 2
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = soh.12556 } # Victory, gained useful insight
		}
		
		else = {
			character_event = { id = soh.12558 } # Victory, gained useful insight and impressed society
		}
	}
}

		death = {
			death_reason = death_lab_accident
		}
		
		event_target:alchemist_to_one_up = {
			character_event = { id = soh.12541 } # Inform rival
		}
	}
}

### Book purchase opportunity
			
# Book tombola
character_event = {
	id = soh.12561
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	has_dlc = "Mystics"
	is_in_society = yes
	min_age = 16
	
	trigger = {
		society_member_of = the_alchemists
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		
		OR = {
			AND = {
				has_global_flag = zhouyi_cantong_qi_exists
				NOT = {
					has_artifact = zhouyi_cantong_qi
				}
			}
			AND = {
				has_global_flag = baopuzi_exists
				NOT = {
					has_artifact = baopuzi
				}
			}
			AND = {
				has_global_flag = danjing_yaojue_exists
				NOT = {
					has_artifact = danjing_yaojue
				}
			}
			AND = {
				has_global_flag = hongbao_yuanbi_shu_exists
				NOT = {
					has_artifact = hongbao_yuanbi_shu
				}
			}
		}
		
		wealth >= 100
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			25 = {
				trigger = {
					has_global_flag = zhouyi_cantong_qi_exists
					NOT = {
						has_artifact = zhouyi_cantong_qi
					}
				}
				
				character_event = { id = soh.12562 } # Actual event
			}
			
			25 = {
				trigger = {
					has_global_flag = baopuzi_exists
					NOT = {
						has_artifact = baopuzi
					}
				}
				
				character_event = { id = soh.12563 } # Actual event
			}
			
			25 = {
				trigger = {
					has_global_flag = danjing_yaojue_exists
					NOT = {
						has_artifact = danjing_yaojue
					}
				}
				
				character_event = { id = soh.12564 } # Actual event
			}
			
			25 = {
				trigger = {
					has_global_flag = hongbao_yuanbi_shu_exists
					NOT = {
						has_artifact = hongbao_yuanbi_shu
					}
				}
				
				character_event = { id = soh.12565 } # Actual event
			}
		}
	}
}

# Zhouyi Cantong Qi purchase opportunity
character_event = {
	id = soh.12562
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12562
	picture = GFX_evt_market
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_economy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12562 # Deal!
		
		wealth = -100
		
		add_artifact = zhouyi_cantong_qi
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			new_artifact = {
				set_creation_date = 1.1.1 # Unknown
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = erudite
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = scholar
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12562 # No deal!
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = greedy
			}

				factor = 10
				trait = content
			}
		}
	}

### Mad alchemist might leave a potion with a "Drink me!" label lying around

# Do you want to leave a potion lying somewhere in case someone unwittingly volunteers to take part in a scientific experiment?
character_event = {
	id = soh.12566
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12566
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	has_dlc = "Mystics"
	only_rulers = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	min_age = 16
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		society_member_of = the_alchemists
		
		OR = {
			trait = lunatic
			trait = possessed
		}
		
		any_artifact = {
			count >= 2
			has_artifact_flag = ingredient
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_artifact = {
			limit = {
				has_artifact_flag = ingredient
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = target_ingredient
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12566 # Yes!
		
		event_target:target_ingredient = {
			destroy_artifact = yes
		}
		
		character_event = { id = soh.12567 days = 10 random = 5 } # Determine outcome
		
		character_event = { id = soh.12581 days = 20 } # Cleanup
		
		set_character_flag = do_not_disturb
		
		set_character_flag = brewing_potion
		
		set_character_flag = drink_me
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = ambitious
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12566 # No!
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = content
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = arbitrary
			}
		}
	}
}

# Determine what happens
character_event = {
	id = soh.12567
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		society_member_of = the_alchemists
		is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
	}
	
	immediate = {
		clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb
		clr_character_flag = brewing_potion
		
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_landed = no
				prisoner = no
				is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
				is_adult = no
				OR = {
					is_dumb_trigger = yes
					age < 6
					trait = trusting
				}
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = potion_child
		}
		
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_landed = no
				prisoner = no
				is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
				is_adult = yes
				OR = {
					AND = {
						is_dumb_trigger = yes
						NOT = {
							trait = paranoid
						}
					}
					trait = trusting
				}
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = potion_courtier
		}
		
		random_list = {
			100 = {
				character_event = { id = soh.12568 } # Potion gone (thrown out by servants)
			}
			
			25 = {
				character_event = { id = soh.12569 } # Servant drank potion and died
			}
			
			25 = {
				character_event = { id = soh.12570 } # Servant drank potion and is now feeling unwell
			}
			
			25 = {
				character_event = { id = soh.12571 } # Servant drank potion and it didn't do anything
			}
			
			25 = {
				character_event = { id = soh.12572 } # Servant drank potion and is now feeling better
			}
			
			10 = {
				trigger = {
					event_target:potion_child = {
						always = yes
					}
				}
				
				character_event = { id = soh.12573 } # Child drank potion and died
			}
			
			10 = {
				trigger = {
					event_target:potion_child = {
						always = yes
					}
				}
				
				character_event = { id = soh.12574 } # Child drank potion is now feeling unwell
			}
			
			10 = {
				trigger = {
					event_target:potion_child = {
						always = yes
					}
				}
				
				character_event = { id = soh.12575 } # Child drank potion and it had no effect
			}
			
			10 = {
				trigger = {
					event_target:potion_child = {
						always = yes
						OR = {
							character_disease_trigger = yes
							has_symptom_trigger = yes
							has_minor_disease_trigger = yes
						}
					}
				}
				
				character_event = { id = soh.12576 } # Child drank potion and is now feeling better
			}
			
			10 = {
				trigger = {
					event_target:potion_courtier = {
						always = yes
					}
				}
				
				character_event = { id = soh.12577 } # Courtier drank potion and died
			}
			
			10 = {
				trigger = {
					event_target:potion_courtier = {
						always = yes
					}
				}
				
				character_event = { id = soh.12578 } # Courtier drank potion is now feeling unwell
			}
			
			10 = {
				trigger = {
					event_target:potion_courtier = {
						always = yes
					}
				}
				
				character_event = { id = soh.12579 } # Courtier drank potion and it had no effect
			}
			
			10 = {
				trigger = {
					event_target:potion_courtier = {
						always = yes
						OR = {
							character_disease_trigger = yes
							has_symptom_trigger = yes
							has_minor_disease_trigger = yes
						}
					}
				}
				
				character_event = { id = soh.12580 } # Courtier drank potion and is now feeling better
			}
		}
	}
}

### Mad alchemist might get to brew "super serum"

# Attempt to brew "super serum"?
character_event = {
	id = soh.12582
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12582
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	has_dlc = "Mystics"
	only_rulers = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	min_age = 16
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		society_member_of = the_alchemists
		
		OR = {
			trait = lunatic
			trait = possessed
		}
		
		any_artifact = {
			count >= 5
			has_artifact_flag = ingredient
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12582 # Yes!
		
		any_artifact = {
			limit = {
				has_artifact_flag = ingredient
			}
			
			destroy_artifact = yes
		}
		
		set_character_flag = brewing_super_serum
		
		set_character_flag = brewing_potion
		
		set_character_flag = do_not_disturb
			
		character_event = { id = soh.12583 days = 10 random = 5 } # Serum is ready
		
		character_event = { id = soh.12634 days = 20 } # Cleanup
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOT = {
					trait = content
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = slothful
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12582 # No!
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOT = {
					trait = ambitious
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = diligent
			}
		}
	}
	
	after = {
		any_artifact = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_artifact_flag = target_ingredient_1
					has_artifact_flag = target_ingredient_2
				}
			}
			
			clr_artifact_flag = target_ingredient_1
			clr_artifact_flag = target_ingredient_2
		}
		
		clr_character_flag = considering_potion
	}
}

# Serum is redy, who should drink it?
character_event = {
	id = soh.12583
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12583
	picture = GFX_evt_experiment
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
		society_member_of = the_alchemists
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
				prisoner = yes
				is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
				is_maimed_trigger = no
				NOT = {
					has_character_flag = mad_scientist_experiment
				}
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				combat_rating >= 100
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				combat_rating >= 75
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				combat_rating >= 50
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				combat_rating >= 25
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				combat_rating >= 0
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = serum_prisoner
		}
		
		random_courtier_or_vassal = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				is_maimed_trigger = no
				has_minor_title = title_commander
				NOR = {
					is_rival = ROOT
					has_character_flag = mad_scientist_experiment
				}
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				combat_rating >= 100
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				combat_rating >= 75
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				combat_rating >= 50
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				combat_rating >= 25
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				combat_rating >= 0
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = serum_commander
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:serum_prisoner = {
				always = yes
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12583 # I will force a prisoner to drink it!
		
		event_target:serum_prisoner = {
			character_event = { id = soh.12584 } # The prisoner gets the serum
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					is_rival = event_target:serum_prisoner
					NOT = {
						trait = trusting
					}
					is_dumb_trigger = no
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				is_smart_trigger = yes
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				is_friend = event_target:serum_prisoner
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				is_lover = event_target:serum_prisoner
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:serum_commander = {
				always = yes
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12583 # One of my commanders will drink it!
		
		event_target:serum_commander = {
			character_event = { id = soh.12603 } # The commander is offered the serum
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					is_rival = event_target:serum_commander
					NOT = {
						trait = trusting
					}
					is_dumb_trigger = no
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				is_smart_trigger = yes
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				is_friend = event_target:serum_commander
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				is_lover = event_target:serum_commander
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTC_SOH_12583 # I will drink it myself
		
		character_event = { id = soh.12624 } # Determine outcome
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOT = {
					trait = craven
					NOT = {
						trait = ambitious
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = brave
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTD_SOH_12583 # Actually, this is a terrible idea!
		
		prestige = -100
		
		clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb
		clr_character_flag = brewing_potion
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					trait = brave
					trait = ambitious
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = content
			}
		}
	}
}

# Are you looking for ingredients, knowledge, valuables, or secrets?

		text = EVTDESCA_SOH_12648
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_character_flag = lab_entered
			}
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCB_SOH_12648
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = lab_entered
		}
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_pagan
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12648 # I'll look for ingredients!
		
		character_event = { id = soh.12649 days = 2 random = 5 } # Determine how it goes
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOT = {
					any_artifact = {
						count = 5
						has_artifact_flag = ingredient
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				NOT = {
					any_artifact = {
						has_artifact_flag = ingredient
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12648 # I'll look for knowledge!
		
		character_event = { id = soh.12656 days = 2 random = 5 } # Determine how it goes
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = scholar
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				check_variable = {
					which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
					value >= 50
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				check_variable = {
					which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
					value >= 50
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				check_variable = {
					which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
					value < 25
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				check_variable = {
					which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
					value < 25
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTC_SOH_12648 # I'll look for valuables!
		
		character_event = { id = soh.12669 days = 2 random = 5 } # Determine how it goes
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOT = {
					trait = charitable
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = greedy
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_character_flag = secret_lab_not_entered
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTD_SOH_12648 # I'll look for secrets!
		
		character_event = { id = soh.12676 days = 2 random = 5 } # Determine how it goes
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					trait = content
					trait = trusting
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = deceitful
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = ambitious
			}
		}
	}
	
	after = {
		set_character_flag = lab_entered
	}
}

# Looking for ingredients - determine how it goes
character_event = {
	id = soh.12649
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		change_variable = {
			which = alchemist_lab_timer
			value = 1
		}
		
		random_list = {
			1 = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_variable_equal = {
							which = alchemist_lab_timer
							which = alchemist_lab_time_limit
						}
					}
					
					character_event = { id = soh.12651 } # Found a lot of ingredients, timed trap triggered
				}
				
				else = {
					character_event = { id = soh.12650 } # Found a lot of ingredients, timed trap not yet triggered
				}
			}
			
			9 = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_variable_equal = {
							which = alchemist_lab_timer
							which = alchemist_lab_time_limit
						}
					}
					
					character_event = { id = soh.12653 } # Found a decent amount of ingredients, timed trap triggered
				}
				
				else = {
					character_event = { id = soh.12652 } # Found a decent amount of ingredients, timed trap not yet triggered
				}
			}
			
			9 = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_variable_equal = {
							which = alchemist_lab_timer
							which = alchemist_lab_time_limit
						}
					}
					
					character_event = { id = soh.12655 } # Found a few ingredients, timed trap triggered
				}
				
				else = {
					character_event = { id = soh.12654 } # Found a few ingredients, timed trap not yet triggered
				}
			}
			
			1 = {
				trigger = {
					NOT = {
						is_variable_equal = {
							which = alchemist_lab_timer
							which = alchemist_lab_time_limit
						}
					}
				}
				
				change_variable = {
					which = alchemist_lab_timer
					value = 1
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_variable_equal = {
							which = alchemist_lab_timer
							which = alchemist_lab_time_limit
						}
					}
					
					character_event = { id = soh.12655 } # Found a few ingredients, timed trap triggered
				}
				
				else = {
					character_event = { id = soh.12654 } # Found a few ingredients, timed trap not yet triggered
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Looking for valuables - determine how it goes
character_event = {
	id = soh.12669
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		change_variable = {
			which = alchemist_lab_timer
			value = 1
		}
		
		random_list = {
			1 = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_variable_equal = {
							which = alchemist_lab_timer
							which = alchemist_lab_time_limit
						}
					}
					
					character_event = { id = soh.12671 } # Found a lot of valuables, timed trap triggered
				}
				
				else = {
					character_event = { id = soh.12670 } # Found a lot of valuables, timed trap not yet triggered
				}
			}
			
			9 = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_variable_equal = {
							which = alchemist_lab_timer
							which = alchemist_lab_time_limit
						}
					}
					
					character_event = { id = soh.12673 } # Found a decent amount of valuables, timed trap triggered
				}
				
				else = {
					character_event = { id = soh.12672 } # Found a decent amount of valuables, timed trap not yet triggered
				}
			}
			
			9 = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_variable_equal = {
							which = alchemist_lab_timer
							which = alchemist_lab_time_limit
						}
					}
					
					character_event = { id = soh.12675 } # Found a few valuables, timed trap triggered
				}
				
				else = {
					character_event = { id = soh.12674 } # Found a few valuables, timed trap not yet triggered
				}
			}
			
			1 = {
				trigger = {
					NOT = {
						is_variable_equal = {
							which = alchemist_lab_timer
							which = alchemist_lab_time_limit
						}
					}
				}
				
				change_variable = {
					which = alchemist_lab_timer
					value = 1
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_variable_equal = {
							which = alchemist_lab_timer
							which = alchemist_lab_time_limit
						}
					}
					
					character_event = { id = soh.12675 } # Found a few valuables, timed trap triggered
				}
				
				else = {
					character_event = { id = soh.12674 } # Found a few valuables, timed trap not yet triggered
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Looking for secrets - determine how it goes
character_event = {
	id = soh.12676
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		change_variable = {
			which = alchemist_lab_timer
			value = 1
		}
		
		random_list = {
			1 = {
				trigger = {
					intrigue >= 20
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_variable_equal = {
							which = alchemist_lab_timer
							which = alchemist_lab_time_limit
						}
					}
					
					character_event = { id = soh.12680 } # Found secret entrance, timed trap triggered
				}
				
				else = {
					character_event = { id = soh.12679 } # Found secret entrance, timed trap not yet triggered
				}
			}
			
			18 = {
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 0.5
					intrigue >= 20
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_variable_equal = {
							which = alchemist_lab_timer
							which = alchemist_lab_time_limit
						}
					}
					
					character_event = { id = soh.12678 } # Found nothing, timed trap triggered
				}
				
				else = {
					character_event = { id = soh.12677 } # Found nothing, timed trap not yet triggered
				}
			}
			
			1 = {
				trigger = {
					NOT = {
						is_variable_equal = {
							which = alchemist_lab_timer
							which = alchemist_lab_time_limit
						}
					}
				}
				
				change_variable = {
					which = alchemist_lab_timer
					value = 1
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_variable_equal = {
							which = alchemist_lab_timer
							which = alchemist_lab_time_limit
						}
					}
					
					character_event = { id = soh.12678 } # Found nothing, timed trap triggered
				}
				
				else = {
					character_event = { id = soh.12677 } # Found nothing, timed trap not yet triggered
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

character_event = {
	id = soh.12695
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			25 = {
				character_event = { id = soh.12696 } # Buried alive
			}
			
			25 = {
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 0.5
					is_maimed_trigger = yes
				}
				
				character_event = { id = soh.12697 } # Maimed, but survived
			}
			
			25 = {
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 0.5
					trait = wounded
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 0.1
					is_maimed_trigger = yes
				}
				
				character_event = { id = soh.12698 } # Wounded, but survived
			}
			25 = {
				trigger = {
					is_maimed_trigger = no
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 0.5
					trait = wounded
				}
				
				character_event = { id = soh.12699 } # Escaped unharmed
			}
		}
	}
}

# Buried alive
character_event = {
	id = soh.12696
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12696
	picture = GFX_evt_pagan
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		any_artifact = {
			limit = {
				has_artifact_flag = found_in_secret_lab
			}
			
			destroy_artifact = yes
		}
		
		while = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					which = alchemist_lab_wealth
					value > 0
				}
			}
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						which = alchemist_lab_wealth
						value > 1000
					}
				}
				
				change_variable = {
					which = alchemist_lab_wealth
					value = -1000
				}
				
				wealth = -1000
			}
			
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						which = alchemist_lab_wealth
						value > 100
					}
				}
				
				change_variable = {
					which = alchemist_lab_wealth
					value = -100
				}
				
				wealth = -100
			}
			
			else = {
				change_variable = {
					which = alchemist_lab_wealth
					value = -10
				}
				
				wealth = -10
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12696 # Not like this!
		
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = everything_discovered_is_lost
		}
		
		death = {
			death_reason = death_accident_wonder_traps
		}
	}
}

### Misc (basically) one-off events

# Expand on non-secret lab?
character_event = {
	id = soh.12701
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12701
	picture = GFX_evt_castle_construction
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	has_dlc = "Mystics"
	only_playable = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	min_age = 16
	
	trigger = {
		society_member_of = the_alchemists
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		NOR = {
			has_character_flag = holy_lab
			has_character_flag = hidden_lab
			has_character_flag = very_hidden_lab
		}
		OR = {
			has_character_modifier = cheap_laboratory
			has_character_modifier = normal_laboratory
			has_character_modifier = good_laboratory
			has_character_modifier = great_laboratory
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12701 # Yes!
		
		wealth = -50
		
		character_event = { id = soh.12702 days = 180 random = 365 } # Lab expansion completed
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOT = {
					trait = content
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = greedy
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = humble
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12701 # No!
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOT = {
					trait = ambitious
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = proud
			}
		}
	}
}

# Expanded lab completed
character_event = {
	id = soh.12702
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12702
	picture = GFX_evt_castle_construction
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		society_member_of = the_alchemists
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12702 # Great!
		
		trigger_switch = {
			on_trigger = has_character_modifier
			
			cheap_laboratory = {
				remove_character_modifier = cheap_laboratory

				add_character_modifier = {
					name = normal_laboratory
					duration = -1
				}
			}
			
			normal_laboratory = {
				remove_character_modifier = normal_laboratory

				add_character_modifier = {
					name = good_laboratory
					duration = -1
				}
			}
			
			good_laboratory = {
				remove_character_modifier = good_laboratory

				add_character_modifier = {
					name = great_laboratory
					duration = -1
				}
			}
			
			great_laboratory = {
				remove_character_modifier = great_laboratory

				add_character_modifier = {
					name = best_laboratory
					duration = -1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# You're now a Renowned Physician
character_event = {
	id = soh.12703
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12703
	picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	has_dlc = "Mystics"
	has_dlc = "Reapers"
	only_playable = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	min_age = 16
	
	trigger = {
		society_member_of = the_alchemists
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		NOT = {
			trait = physician
		}
		check_variable = {
			which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
			value >= 25
		}
		OR = {
			has_minor_title = title_court_physician
			has_minor_title = title_court_alchemist
			society_rank >= 3
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12703 # Excellent!
		
		add_trait = physician
	}
}

	picture = GFX_evt_performing_experiments
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	has_dlc = "Mystics"
	only_playable = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	min_age = 16
	
	trigger = {
		society_member_of = the_alchemists
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		OR = {
			has_character_modifier = cheap_laboratory
			has_character_modifier = normal_laboratory
			has_character_modifier = good_laboratory
			has_character_modifier = great_laboratory
			has_character_modifier = best_laboratory
		}
		any_artifact = {
			has_artifact_flag = ingredient
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_artifact = {
			limit = {
				has_artifact_flag = ingredient
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = artifact_to_destroy
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12705 # Curses!
		
		event_target:artifact_to_destroy = {
			destroy_artifact = yes
		}
	}
}

		society_member_of = the_alchemists
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		has_secret_religion = no
		NOR = {
			trait = zhengyi_taoist
			has_character_flag = india_branch_converted
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12706 # Yes!
		
		add_trait = zhengyi_taoist
		
		set_character_flag = india_branch_converted
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					trait = stubborn
					trait = zealous
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12706 # No!
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
		}
	}
}
