# Share secrets with friend/lover in the RAS for free?
character_event = {
	id = soh.12711
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12711
	picture = GFX_evt_gathering_of_minds
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	has_dlc = "Mystics"
	only_playable = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	min_age = 16
	
	trigger = {
		society_member_of = the_alchemists
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		OR = {
			any_friend = {
				society_member_of = the_alchemists
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				NAND = {
					check_variable = {
						which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
						which = ROOT
					}
					check_variable = {
						which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
						which = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
			any_lover = {
				society_member_of = the_alchemists
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				NAND = {
					check_variable = {
						which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
						which = ROOT
					}
					check_variable = {
						which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
						which = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		OR = {
			check_variable = {
				which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
				value >= 25
			}
			check_variable = {
				which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
				value >= 25
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					any_friend = {
						society_member_of = the_alchemists
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						NAND = {
							check_variable = {
								which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
								which = ROOT
							}
							check_variable = {
								which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
								which = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				random_friend = {
					limit = {
						society_member_of = the_alchemists
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						NAND = {
							check_variable = {
								which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
								which = ROOT
							}
							check_variable = {
								which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
								which = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
					
					save_event_target_as = share_secrets
				}
			}
			
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					any_lover = {
						society_member_of = the_alchemists
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						NAND = {
							check_variable = {
								which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
								which = ROOT
							}
							check_variable = {
								which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
								which = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				random_lover = {
					limit = {
						society_member_of = the_alchemists
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						NAND = {
							check_variable = {
								which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
								which = ROOT
							}
							check_variable = {
								which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
								which = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
					
					save_event_target_as = share_secrets
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12711 # Yes!
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			set_variable = {
				which = alchemist_variable_increase_healing
				which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
			}
			
			set_variable = {
				which = alchemist_variable_increase_poison
				which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
			}
		}
		
		event_target:share_secrets = {
			character_event = { id = soh.12712 } # Inform friend/lover
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = proud
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = envious
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = paranoid
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12711 # No!
		
		random = {
			chance = 5
			add_trait_partially_paranoid_effect = yes
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = humble
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = kind
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = trusting
			}
		}
	}
}

# Inform friend/lover
character_event = {
	id = soh.12712
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12712
	picture = GFX_evt_china_diplomatic_success
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12712 # Thank you!
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						check_variable = {
							which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
							which = FROM
						}
					}
				}
				
				set_variable = {
					which = alchemist_variable_increase_healing
					which = FROM
				}
				
				subtract_variable = {
					which = alchemist_variable_increase_healing
					which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
				}
			}
			
			set_variable = {
				which = loop_counter
				value = 0
			}
			
			while = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						which = alchemist_variable_increase_healing
						value > 0
					}
					check_variable = {
						which = loop_counter
						value < 10
					}
				}
				
				change_variable = {
					which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
					value = 1
				}
				
				change_variable = {
					which = alchemist_variable_increase_healing
					value = -1
				}
				
				change_variable = {
					which = loop_counter
					value = 1
				}
			}
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						check_variable = {
							which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
							which = FROM
						}
					}
				}
				
				set_variable = {
					which = alchemist_variable_increase_poison
					which = FROM
				}
				
				subtract_variable = {
					which = alchemist_variable_increase_poison
					which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
				}
			}
			
			set_variable = {
				which = loop_counter
				value = 0
			}
			
			while = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						which = alchemist_variable_increase_poison
						value > 0
					}
					check_variable = {
						which = loop_counter
						value < 10
					}
				}
				
				change_variable = {
					which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
					value = 1
				}
				
				change_variable = {
					which = alchemist_variable_increase_poison
					value = -1
				}
				
				change_variable = {
					which = loop_counter
					value = 1
				}
			}
		}
		
		opinion = {
			who = FROM
			name = opinion_shared_insights
		}
	}
}

# Share insights with the whole society for society currency?
character_event = {
	id = soh.12713
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12713
	picture = GFX_evt_gathering_of_minds
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	has_dlc = "Mystics"
	only_playable = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	min_age = 16
	
	trigger = {
		society_member_of = the_alchemists
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		the_alchemists = {
			any_society_member = {
				NOT = {
					is_rival = ROOT
				}
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				NAND = {
					check_variable = {
						which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
						which = ROOT
					}
					check_variable = {
						which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
						which = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		OR = {
			check_variable = {
				which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
				value >= 25
			}
			check_variable = {
				which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
				value >= 25
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12713 # Yes!
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			set_variable = {
				which = alchemist_variable_increase_healing
				which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
			}
			
			set_variable = {
				which = alchemist_variable_increase_poison
				which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
			}
		}
		
		the_alchemists = {
			any_society_member = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						is_rival = ROOT
					}
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					NAND = {
						check_variable = {
							which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
							which = ROOT
						}
						check_variable = {
							which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
							which = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
				
				ROOT = {
					add_society_currency_tiny_effect = yes
				}
				
				character_event = { id = soh.12714 } # Inform other members
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = proud
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = envious
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = paranoid
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12713 # No!
		
		random = {
			chance = 25
			add_trait_partially_proud_effect = yes
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = humble
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = kind
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = trusting
			}
		}
	}
}

# Non-mad alchemist might distance themself from mad alchemist friend/lover
character_event = {
	id = soh.12715
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12715
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	has_dlc = "Mystics"
	only_playable = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	min_age = 16
	
	trigger = {
		society_member_of = the_alchemists
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		OR = {
			any_friend = {
				society_member_of = the_alchemists
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				OR = {
					trait = lunatic
					trait = possessed
				}
			}
			any_lover = {
				society_member_of = the_alchemists
				is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
				OR = {
					trait = lunatic
					trait = possessed
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					any_friend = {
						society_member_of = the_alchemists
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						OR = {
							trait = lunatic
							trait = possessed
						}
					}
				}
				
				random_friend = {
					limit = {
						society_member_of = the_alchemists
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						OR = {
							trait = lunatic
							trait = possessed
						}
					}
					
					save_event_target_as = former_associate
				}
			}
			
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					any_lover = {
						society_member_of = the_alchemists
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						OR = {
							trait = lunatic
							trait = possessed
						}
					}
				}
				
				random_lover = {
					limit = {
						society_member_of = the_alchemists
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						OR = {
							trait = lunatic
							trait = possessed
						}
					}
					
					save_event_target_as = former_associate
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12715 # Yes!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_friend = event_target:former_associate
			}
			
			tooltip = {
				remove_friend = event_target:former_associate
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_lover = event_target:former_associate
			}
			
			tooltip = {
				remove_lover = event_target:former_associate
			}
		}
		
		add_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
		
		event_target:former_associate = {
			character_event = { id = soh.12716 } # Inform friend/lover
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				opinion = {
					who = event_target:former_associate
					value < 50
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				opinion = {
					who = event_target:former_associate
					value >= 25
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12715 # No!
		
		detract_society_currency_minor_effect = yes
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				opinion = {
					who = event_target:former_associate
					value >= -50
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				opinion = {
					who = event_target:former_associate
					value < -25
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Inform mad alchemist
character_event = {
	id = soh.12716
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12716
	picture = GFX_evt_china_diplomatic_incident
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12716 # You'll regret this! You'll ALL regret this!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_friend = event_target:former_associate
			}
			
			remove_friend = event_target:former_associate
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_lover = event_target:former_associate
			}
			
			remove_lover = event_target:former_associate
		}
	}
}

# Genius alchemist comes across as insufferable
character_event = {
	id = soh.12717
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12717
	picture = GFX_evt_chinese_bureaucrat
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	has_dlc = "Mystics"
	only_playable = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	min_age = 16
	
	trigger = {
		society_member_of = the_alchemists
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		is_smart_trigger = yes
		NOR = {
			trait = humble
			trait = shy
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12717 # So what?
		
		prestige = 100
		
		detract_society_currency_tiny_effect = yes
		
		random = {
			chance = 25
			add_trait_partially_proud_effect = yes
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOT = {
					trait = humble
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_SOH_12717 # Perhaps I should change my ways...
		
		prestige = -100
		
		add_society_currency_tiny_effect = yes
		
		random = {
			chance = 25
			add_trait_partially_humble_effect = yes
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					trait = proud
					trait = stubborn
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Genius alchemist might cross the fine line between genius and madness

	id = soh.12718
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12718
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	has_dlc = "Mystics"
	only_playable = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	min_age = 16
	
	trigger = {
		society_member_of = the_alchemists
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		NOR = {
			trait = lunatic
			trait = possessed
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12718 # I think that line might be behind me...
		
		random = {
			chance = 25
			add_trait_lunatic_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12722
	picture = GFX_evt_chinese_bureaucrat

	has_dlc = "Mystics"
	only_playable = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	min_age = 16

		society_member_of = the_alchemists
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		NOT = {
			trait = proud
		}
		check_variable = {
			which = alchemist_healing_knowledge
			value >= 10
		}
		check_variable = {
			which = alchemist_poison_knowledge
			value >= 10
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12722 # So be it!
		
		add_trait_partially_proud_effect = yes
	}

# Alchemist becomes Stubborn
character_event = {
	id = soh.12732
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12732
	picture = GFX_evt_china_diplomatic_incident
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	has_dlc = "Mystics"
	only_playable = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	min_age = 16
	
	trigger = {
		society_member_of = the_alchemists
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		NOT = {
			trait = stubborn
		}
		OR = {
			trait = diligent
			trait = patient
			trait = ambitious
		}
		NOR = {
			trait = slothful
			trait = content
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12732 # So be it!
		
		add_trait_stubborn_effect = yes
	}
}

# Alchemist and Hermetic inside the same realm bond over shared interest - alchemist
character_event = {
	id = soh.12736
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12736
	picture = GFX_evt_experiment
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	has_dlc = "Mystics"
	only_playable = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	min_age = 16
	
	trigger = {
		society_member_of = the_alchemists
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		employer = {
			top_liege = {
				any_courtier_or_vassal = {
					is_adult = yes
					society_member_of = hermetics
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					NOR = {
						is_friend = ROOT
						is_lover = ROOT
						is_rival = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		employer = {
			top_liege = {
				random_courtier_or_vassal = {
					limit = {
						is_adult = yes
						society_member_of = hermetics
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						NOR = {
							is_friend = ROOT
							is_lover = ROOT
							is_rival = ROOT
						}
					}
					
					save_event_target_as = new_friend
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12736 # We have so much in common!
		
		add_friend = event_target:new_friend
		
		event_target:new_friend = {
			character_event = { id = soh.12737 } # Inform new friend
		}
	}
}

# Alchemist and Hermetic inside the same realm bond over shared interest - Hermetic

# Alchemist and Hermetic inside the same realm become rivals - alchemist
character_event = {
	id = soh.12738
	desc = EVTDESC_SOH_12738
	picture = GFX_evt_rival_rulers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	has_dlc = "Mystics"
	only_playable = yes
	is_in_society = yes
	min_age = 16
	
	trigger = {
		society_member_of = the_alchemists
		is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
		employer = {
			top_liege = {
				any_courtier_or_vassal = {
					is_adult = yes
					society_member_of = hermetics
					is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					NOR = {
						is_friend = ROOT
						is_lover = ROOT
						is_rival = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		employer = {
			top_liege = {
				random_courtier_or_vassal = {
					limit = {
						is_adult = yes
						society_member_of = hermetics
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
						NOR = {
							is_friend = ROOT
							is_lover = ROOT
							is_rival = ROOT
						}
					}
					
					save_event_target_as = new_rival
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_SOH_12738 # You are not a proper alchemist!
		
		add_rival = event_target:new_rival
		
		event_target:new_rival = {
			character_event = { id = soh.12739 } # Inform new friend
		}
	}
}
